# SGW_Harset_Dev_Master_v1

Markdown rendering of `SGW_Harset_Dev_Master_v1.xlsx` from the SGW Claude Server Handoff Pack v1.2.
Generated by `xlsx_to_md.py`; cell values only (no formulas, formatting or comments).
Sheet order and headers are preserved verbatim. Evidence labels inside the
sheets control authority, per the pack's `docs/SOURCE_POLICY.md`.

## 00_README

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Workbook | SGW Harset — Dev Master v1 |
| Scope | Everything recoverable that materially concerns Harset: main world, Command Center, Market, Storage Room, local missions, Harset handoffs/returns, faction-specific progression, gate travel, NPCs, items, interactions, Kismet/audio references, map files and implementation gaps. |
| Factions | Goa'uld / Loyalist Jaffa / OP-Core Human are tracked separately. Shared or unclear records are not silently assigned to a faction. |
| Progression rule | Harset visits are separated by explicit OFFWORLD pause blocks. Where a mission step explicitly hands the player back to Harset, that handoff is recorded. Where only the campaign aid supplies the next planet, the OFFWORLD block is clearly marked as planning/reconstruction evidence. |
| First arrival | OP-Core Human + Loyalist Jaffa arrive from the Castle chain. Goa'uld first-Harset origin is shown from the progression aid and is not promoted to raw-source canon. |
| Important correction | The progression aid skips a Loyalist-Jaffa Harset stop after Tollana, but raw Mission 1643 / Step 4870 explicitly sends the player to Moh'katan on Harset. Raw mission evidence wins. |
| Worlds | Harset = World 57; Harset_CmdCenter = 68; Harset_Market = 69; Harset_StorageRm = 70. |
| Stargate | Harset Stargate = ID 3 / World 57. Exact cooked transform and address are in 13_Stargate_Travel. |
| Local mission count | 30 |
| Harset-linked mission count | 74 |
| Harset map/reference file count | 122 |
| Evidence labels | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED; USER-CONFIRMED; RECONSTRUCTION / INFERENCE; PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING. |
| Authority rule | Raw cooked client/runtime data outranks modified mission spreadsheets and the progression planning aid. Missing coordinates, spawns, region bounds, inventories, minigame semantics and grant conditions remain visible as gaps. |
| Map limitation | File index proves Harset map/interior files exist across SGW_Maps_003(1) and SGW_Maps_004(2), but exact actor transforms/trigger boxes require raw binary/server inspection. |
| Sample-mission anchor | Mission 742 Giving the Walls Ears is especially well documented: Anat → Petbe/Jaffa disguise → Scarab devices in Jaffa area → Nerus/Anat Command Center context. Improvised tutorial placements are not canonical. |
| Snapshot | 2026-09-18; built from SGW_RECONSTRUCTION_MASTER_HANDOFF and retained Harset research workbooks. |

_16 data rows, 2 columns._

## 26_Sources

| Source | Type | Authority | Harset contribution | Caution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| structured/CookedDataMissions.jsonl | Raw cooked SourceCache | PRIMARY | Local Harset missions, crossworld Harset return steps, objectives/tasks, levels and labels. | Client cache omits some server grant/region/script semantics. |
| structured/CookedDataDialogs.jsonl | Raw cooked SourceCache | PRIMARY | Harset mission dialogue screens, speaker IDs and faction narrative. | SpeakerID alone does not prove physical spawn/template. |
| structured/CookedInteractionSet.jsonl | Raw cooked SourceCache | PRIMARY | Harset mission interaction-set maps/topics and dialogue linkage. | Interaction flags semantics still need runtime/server interpretation. |
| structured/CookedDataItems.jsonl | Raw cooked SourceCache | PRIMARY | Mission items such as Jaffa Disguise, Scarab devices, Ration Card, RepliTech, Lan'toc, Book variants. | Item existence/name does not prove placement/inventory transaction. |
| structured/CookedDataAbilities.jsonl + CookedDataEffects.jsonl | Raw cooked SourceCache | PRIMARY | Harset-specific success/fail dialogue hooks, Dawson state, Moles/Rin'la/Lan'toc effects. | Effect description does not by itself prove full task trigger. |
| structured/CookedDataKismetSetEvent.jsonl / SeqEvent | Raw cooked SourceCache | PRIMARY | Harset Stargate prefab and d_Harset SoundBankName/Kismet references. | Individual sequence-event semantics require raw map/Kismet audit. |
| structured/WORLDS.json + STARGATES.json | Raw cooked structured data | PRIMARY | World IDs 57/68/69/70 and exact Harset Stargate record. | Does not provide all interior region/travel conditions. |
| indexes/FULL_CLIENT_FILE_INDEX.jsonl | Audited file index | PRIMARY for file presence | Harset main/interior maps, streaming cells, MapData and supplemental reference-file presence. | File presence is not actor placement evidence. |
| Implementing a sample mission.docx (handoff key source) | Technical/reconstruction tutorial | HIGH secondary / mixed | Mission 742 source-backed facts and CommandCenter region example. | Tutorial explicitly improvises some physical placements. |
| SGW_ Milky Way Missions_Dev_Ver.xlsx | Project-era cross-reference | SECONDARY | Harset mission step worksheet, notes/minigame hints. | Useful but not pristine/complete; raw cooked data wins on conflicts. |
| Mission data_Dialogue_Ids.xlsx | Project-era modified working file | SECONDARY | Harset/Tollana dialogue grouping and faction variants. | Not pristine developer authority; verify critical rows against raw data. |
| SGW_ Progression.xlsx | Reconstruction/planning aid | AID ONLY | High-level planet sequence and repeated Harset visits. | Never sole canonical proof; Mission 1643 demonstrates an omission. |
| Items Database.xlsx | Cross-reference export | SECONDARY | Readable mission-item cross-check. | Use raw CookedDataItems for authoritative client record. |
| Planets & Locations.xlsx | Reference workbook | SECONDARY | Location/world naming cross-reference. | Not sufficient for runtime placement. |
| MASTER_SOURCE.md | Project continuity/methodology | PROJECT AUTHORITY | Evidence labels, hierarchy, Harset world facts, Mission 742 example, user-confirmed early faction routes. | Method/source hierarchy, not replacement for raw records. |

## 27_Scope_Exclusions

| Excluded / bounded content | Why | What is retained in Harset workbook | Implementation rule | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Full Tollana runtime | Offworld world/map content. | OFFWORLD pause rows, mission IDs and explicit Harset return handoffs. | Do not copy Tollana NPCs/props/spawns into Harset. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED — OUT OF SCOPE |
| Full Pen-Lai runtime | Offworld world; production UMAP absent in current raw map set. | Route/handoff references only. | Do not invent Pen-Lai geometry on Harset. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED — OUT OF SCOPE |
| Full Dakara runtime | Separate worlds/maps. | Only Harset Command Center return steps / handoffs. | Keep Dakara tasks on Dakara; activate Harset recipients only at return state. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED — OUT OF SCOPE |
| Full Naitac runtime | Separate unfinished world. | Route marker only where user-confirmed/progression requires it. | Do not fabricate Naitac mission implementation inside Harset. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED — OUT OF SCOPE |
| Full Ihpet/Lucia/Men'fa/Hebridan/Beta/Agnos/Tartarus/Yotunheim runtime | Separate worlds/maps/arcs. | Only Harset report/handoff records and OFFWORLD pause blocks. | World-specific targets stay offworld. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED — OUT OF SCOPE |
| Unproven shared-faction grants | Raw mission return exists but faction condition may be absent. | Mission appears in 04 and faction sheets as shared candidate. | Do not give to all three factions without server grant evidence. | PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING |
| Invented NPC/enemy coordinates | Server spawn transforms missing. | Logical region / interior only. | No XYZ labeled canonical unless map/server evidence is recovered. | PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING |
| Invented vendor/black-market inventories | Dialogue/requisitions roles are not inventory tables. | NPC role and interaction evidence only. | Recover inventory tables before populating stock/prices. | PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING |
| Improvised sample-mission placements | Tutorial explicitly improvises some placement. | Mission 742 facts/items/dialog/region example retained. | Do not relabel tutorial improvisation as FireSky original. | RECONSTRUCTION / INFERENCE |
| Automatic merge of duplicate item IDs | Several Harset items have duplicate names/descriptions. | Every raw ItemID retained separately. | Resolve task/script bindings first. | PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING |
| Progression-aid-only transitions | Planning sheet may omit/add coarse stops. | Shown only as USER-CONFIRMED or RECONSTRUCTION context. | Explicit mission handoffs override coarse diagram. | PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING |
